- **habit-design**: Designs one habit at a time as cue → routine → reward: writes an implementation intention ("when X happens, I will Y" — Gollwitzer's if-then effect), stacks the routine onto an existing stable habit, engineers friction so the good path gets one step shorter and the bad path one step longer, starts below the failure threshold and scales only after stability, tracks presence rather than streaks (a missed day is data, not a moral event), and schedules a renegotiation date so the habit is kept, resized, or retired deliberately. - **habit-four-laws-audit**: 四定律诊断工具：把一个"做不到的好习惯"或"戒不掉的坏习惯"拆成提示-渴望-反应-奖励四环，找出卡住的那一环（瓶颈），再给对应工具——好习惯用显而易见/有吸引力/简便易行/令人愉悦，坏习惯反向用隐形/没吸引力/困难/不愉悦，重点是环境设计而非意志力。用户能说出一个具体行为、且它在固定场景里反复做不到或戒不掉时激活。 触发信号："道理都懂就是做不到""戒不掉睡前刷手机""总是忘了做""一回家就瘫沙发""明明知道不好还是忍不住""靠意志力撑不住"。 不触发场景：还没开始 → habit-starter-design；断了、没效果、做腻了 → habit-streak-plateau；"我就是这种人" → habit-identity-votes；工作时一无聊就分心 → deepwork-embrace-boredom；成瘾、进食障碍 → 去医院精神心理科或戒烟门诊；想改伴侣的习惯 → eft-demon-dialogues。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/fenxixitong](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/fenxixitong/tree/HEAD/skills/habit-four-laws-audit))

- **habit-streak-keeper**: Tracks the user's daily habits as streaks in memory and nudges them on Telegram in the evening when a live streak is about to break. The user marks a habit done with "did: [habit]" (e.g. "did: gym"); the agent counts consecutive days per habit, and each evening pings any habit done yesterday but not yet today — so a 12-day streak doesn't quietly die from one forgotten day. Stays silent when every active habit is already done for the day. ([nearai/ironhub](https://github.com/nearai/ironhub/tree/HEAD/skills/habit-streak-keeper) · ★8)
- **habit-streak-plateau**: 习惯维持工具：处理已经开始之后的三种掉队——①中断了（用"不连续两次断"快速回归，避免全有或全无）；②做了很久看不到效果（潜力平台期，检查是不是指标选错）；③做腻了（金发姑娘难度 + 职业者心态 + 定期复盘）。配合轻量的习惯追踪。习惯已经建立过、现在断了、停滞或厌倦时激活。 触发信号："断了一周就不想再捡起来""坚持一个月了体重一点没变""每天都一样，越来越没劲""打卡断了就全盘放弃""感觉自己在原地踏步""要不要换个方法重新来"。 不触发场景：习惯还没开始 → habit-starter-design；从一开始就做不到 → habit-four-laws-audit；"我是不是根本不是这种人" → habit-identity-votes；一失败就觉得自己不行 → mindset-reaction-diagnosis；专注力训练 → deepwork-embrace-boredom；低落两周以上 → psyche 或医院精神心理科；为冲平台期极端节食、过度运动、催吐 → 先叫停并转医院营养科或精神心理科。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/fenxixitong](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/fenxixitong/tree/HEAD/skills/habit-streak-plateau))
